## Document 010: 158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～

Source PDF: all report pdf/158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～.pdf

### Page 1

Investment Navigator

2026 年 10 月 22 日

1/6 
158 円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 
～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の"三つの分岐点"が方向性を決める～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ ドル円相場は、7 月末の日米協調介入、9 月上旬の急激な円高、9 月の日米同時利上げを経て、
10 月に入ってからは1 ドル＝158 円前後の狭いレンジでの推移が続いている。円先物市場に
おける非商業部門の建玉は、9 月前半に過去に例のない規模で円の売り越しから買い越しへ
と転じた。しかし、その後は買い越し幅が縮小し、売建玉が再び増え始めている。投機筋の
円買い意欲は弱まりつつあると言えよう。 
○ 期待インフレ率 （BEI） で実質化した日米の実質金利差は、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債のいず
れでも 2％ポイントを超える米国優位が続いている。名目金利差は縮小したものの、米国の
金利上昇は実質金利の上昇、日本のは期待インフレ率の上昇によるところが大きい。このた
め、実質ベースのドル優位は崩れていない。 
○ 当面の焦点は、①11 月 3 日の米中間選挙、②選挙後のイラン情勢、③日銀の 12 月利上げと
その後のペース、の三つである。民主党が上下両院を制し、イラン情勢が沈静化に向かい、
日銀が利上げ路線を明確にすれば、円高方向への転換が視野に入る。逆の組み合わせとなれ
ば、再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が強まる展開も否定できない。

1. 投機筋の円ポジションは"円売り一色"から変化し、158 円を挟んで膠着が続くドル円相場 
ドル円相場は、方向感を欠いた推移が続いている。10 月入り後の値動きは、高値が158.94 円
（10 月14 日） 、安値が156.99 円（10 月2 日）と、月間の値幅は2 円弱にとどまる。 
もっとも、ここに至るまでの道のりは平坦ではなかった。4 月末から5 月の大型連休中には、
160 円台後半まで進んだ円安に対して、政府・日銀が総額11.7 兆円の円買い介入を実施した。そ
れでも円安基調は止まらず、7 月 28 日には163.91 円（ECB参照値）と、約40 年ぶりの円安水準を
つけた。これに対し、7 月 31 日には日米当局が28 年ぶりとなる円買い協調介入に踏み切った。介
入額は 15 兆3993 億円に達し、年初からの累計は27 兆 1342 億円と暦年で過去最大を更新した。 
その後は円高へと自律的な反発も見られた。9 月2～3 日には、日銀の追加利上げ観測の強まり
や米雇用関連指標の下振れを受けてドル円は1 日で3 円以上下落、9 日には153 円台まで円高が進
んだ。この局面では介入実績は無く、ジャクソンホール会議でのウォーシュFRB議長の発言をきっ

### Page 2

2/6 
かけに積み上がったドル買い・円売りの持ち高が、損切りを迫られた結果と考えられる。 
ところが、その後は再び円安に押し戻された。FRBは 9 月 16 日に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切
り、政策金利を3.75～4.00％とした。日銀も18 日に政策金利を1.25％へ引き上げたものの、日
米がそろって利上げしたことで金利差は大きく変わらず、ドル円は158 円台へと戻っている。 
為替市場における"投機的な動き"の指標とされるのが、円先物市場における非商業部門（主に
ヘッジファンドなどとされる）の建玉である。その推移を見ると、ここ数ヶ月で大きな変化が生
じていることがわかる（図表1）。 
6 月 2 日時点の円売建玉は24 万 4416 枚と、円キャリートレードがブームだった2007 年以来の
高水準に達していた。差引（ネット）でも12 万9567 枚の売り越しだった。その後も、9 月1 日時
点では 9 万2227 枚の売り越しと、円売り超の状態が続いていた。 
しかし、9 月前半の急激な円高を境に状況は一変し、9 月 15 日時点では差引が12 万 0359 枚の
買い越しへと転じ、わずか2 週間で円買い方向に21 万枚強も振れた。この振れ幅は、統計をさか
のぼれる1986 年以降で最大である。同時点の円買建玉（23 万 7951 枚）も過去最大を記録した。 
もっとも、その後は買い越し幅の縮小が続いている。10 月13 日時点では、買建玉19 万 1256 枚
に対し売建玉14 万 1049 枚で、差引の買い越しは5 万0207 枚とピークの半分以下に縮んだ。売建
玉は 9 月末の12 万枚台から14 万枚台へと再び増えており、円売りの持ち高が積み上がり始めて
いる兆しもうかがえる。投資主体別に見ると、レバレッジド・ファンドは10 月 13 日時点で3 万
3673 枚の円売り越しとなっており、ヘッジファンドの円買い意欲がすでに後退していることを示
している。 
足元の売建玉は、過去の介入局面のように当局が介入を急がなければならない水準にはない。
一方で、9 月に積み上がった円の買い持ちの解消そのものが、円安圧力として作用している面もあ
ると考えられる。

2.  実質金利差から見たドル円相場
為替相場の方向性を最終的に決めるのは、日米の金融政策と、それを反映した金利差である。
そこで、日米の実質金利差を、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債の3 つの年限で見てみる（図表2） 。こ
こでは、実質化に用いる物価として、市場が織り込む期待インフレ率であるBEI（ブレーク・イー
ブン・インフレ率、10 年債）を用いた。足元の消費者物価は、米国では原油高で押し上げられ、
日本では電気・ガス料金支援で押し下げられており、実勢を見誤りやすいためである。
名目で見ると、政策金利は米FF金利の誘導目標が3.75～4.00％、日本の無担保コール翌日物が
1.25％である。10 月 21 日時点の国債利回りは、米国が2 年債4.79％・10 年債 5.13％、日本が2
年債 1.886％・10 年債2.882％となっている。一方、10 年BEIは米国が2.35％（10 月20 日） 、日
本が 2.1～2.2％程度と、ほぼ同水準である。これを差し引いて実質化すると、筆者の試算ではお

おむね次のようになる。
✓ 政策金利：米国＋1.5～1.6％程度、日本▲0.9～1.0％程度（米国優位・差は約2.5％ポイント）
✓ 2 年債：米国＋2.4％程度、日本▲0.3％程度（米国優位・差は約2.7％ポイント）
✓ 10 年債：米国＋2.8％程度、日本＋0.7％程度（米国優位・差は約2.1％ポイント）
名目で見れば、日本の10 年債利回りが9 月下旬に3.115％と約 30 年ぶりの水準に達するなど、
日米金利差は縮小してきた。しかし、BEIで実質化すると景色は異なる。すべての年限で米国の実
質金利が2％ポイント以上も上回っており、ドル優位の構図はほとんど崩れていないのである。
その背景には、日米で金利上昇の"中身"が異なることがある。米国では、昨年末から長期金利
が約 1％ポイント上昇したが、BEIは 2.25％から2.35％へとほぼ横ばいにとどまった。名目金利の
注：BEIで実質化

4/6 
上昇は、ほぼそのまま実質金利の上昇となり、10 年物の実質利回りは9 月末に 2.93％と2008 年
以来の高水準に達した。これに対し日本では、BEIが過去最高圏の2％台まで上昇しており、名目
金利の上昇の相応の部分を期待インフレ率の上昇が占めている。実際、9 月の日銀の利上げ後も
BEIはむしろ上昇した。日本の金利上昇は"インフレ期待による金利上昇"の色彩が濃く、実質金利
の上昇は限られていると言えよう。 
日本の政策金利は、実質では依然として▲1％近いマイナス圏にあり、金融環境はなお緩和的
だ。日銀の利上げや介入にもかかわらず円安の流れが変わらなかったのは、こうした実質金利差
の構図で説明できる面が大きい。逆に言えば、日本の期待インフレ率が2％近辺で落ち着いたまま
利上げが進むか、米国の実質金利が低下に転じない限り、本格的な円高への転換は難しいと考え
られる。 
なお、日本の物価連動国債は発行残高が約10 兆円と小さく、BEIには流動性の影響も含まれ
る。政策金利や2 年債を10 年BEIで実質化するのも簡便的な手法である点には留意が必要だ。

3.  中間選挙の結果次第で変わるドルの行方 
11 月3 日には米国の中間選挙が行われる。1934 年以降の23 回の中間選挙のうち、現職大統領
の政党が上下両院とも議席を増やしたのはわずか2 回しかない。今回も、ロイター通信の世論調
査ではトランプ大統領の支持率が過去最低の31％まで落ち込み、投票先では民主党47％、共和党
35％と 12 ポイントの差がついている。選挙予測サイトのディシジョン・デスクHQは、民主党が下
院を制する確率を78％、上院を制する確率を56％と見ている。 
結果ごとに、ドル円相場への影響を整理すると次のようになる。 
① 民主党が上下両院を制する場合：予算や減税、関税政策に対する議会のチェックが強まり、
財政拡張への期待が後退することで、米長期金利の低下とドル安につながりやすい。FRBの人事に
対する議会の影響力が強まり、金融政策の独立性への懸念が和らぐことも、長期金利の押し下げ
要因となろう。一方で、予算案を巡る対立が先鋭化すれば、政府機関の閉鎖などを通じたリスク
回避の円買いが生じる可能性もある。いずれにしても、ドル円には下押し圧力がかかる公算が大
きい。 
② 下院は民主党、上院は共和党の"ねじれ"となる場合：現在の政策の大枠が維持される一方
で、新たな大型財政出動は難しくなる。相場の反応は限定的で、関心は再び日米の金融政策へと
移ることになろう。 
③ 共和党が両院の過半数を維持する場合：減税や規制緩和など拡張的な政策への期待から、米
長期金利の上昇とドル高が進みやすい。ドル円が再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が一気に高
まる展開も想定される。 
世論調査どおりであれば①ないし②の公算が大きく、ドル円にはどちらかと言えば円高寄りの
材料となろう。ただし、重要なのは選挙後の財政・通商政策や金利見通しの変化であり、選挙直
後の値動きだけで方向性を判断するのは早計だろう。

### Page 5

5/6 
4.  イラン情勢：選挙後の"重し"が外れる 
もう一つの大きな不透明要因がイラン情勢である。2 月 28 日に始まった米国・イスラエルとイ
ランの戦闘は、ホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖と、米国によるイラン港湾の海上封鎖を伴ったまま
長期化している。北海ブレント原油先物は20 日に一時1 バレル＝111.99 ドルと約5 ヶ月ぶりの高
値をつけた。 
原油高は、いくつかの経路でドル高円安に作用している。米国では、ガソリン価格の前年比
34％の上昇などを背景に、9 月の消費者物価が同＋3.7％へと加速し、FRBの追加利上げ観測を支え
ている。日本にとっては、エネルギー輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支が悪化し、実需の円売りが
増える。加えて、地政学リスクの高まりは"有事のドル買い"も誘いやすい。 
注目されるのは、トランプ大統領が「中間選挙前にはイランを攻撃しない」と明言している点
だ。言い換えれば、選挙後はこの制約が外れることになる。選挙後に軍事行動が再開されれば、
原油価格の一段高と米利上げ観測の強まりを通じて、ドル高円安が進むリスクがある。逆に、仲
介国を通じた交渉がまとまり、海峡の再開に向かえば、原油価格の下落とともに米長期金利が低
下し、ドル安円高へと振れやすい。実際、20～21 日には原油安を受けて米長期金利が低下し、ド
ルが売られる場面が見られた。

5.  日米の金融政策：12 月の"同時利上げ"の先が焦点 
FRBは9 月に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切り、年内にもう1 回の利上げを示唆した。10 月14 日
に9 月の消費者物価が前年比3.7％と予想を上回ると、27～28 日のFOMCでの利上げ確率は一時5
割前後まで上昇した。しかし、17 日にウォーシュ議長が10 月会合の判断を予断しない姿勢を示し
たことで、利上げ確率は1 割台半ばまで低下している。市場では「10 月は見送り、12 月に利上
げ」との見方が大勢となった。一部には、12 月が今回の利上げ局面で最後になるとの予想も出て
いる。 
一方、日銀の植田総裁は、9 月の利上げ後に「政策の局面は変化した」と述べ、その後も基調的
な物価上昇率が2％に「かなり近づいている」との認識を示した。そのうえで、 「これ以上上がる
ことは容認できない」とも踏み込んでいる。29～30 日の決定会合では政策金利が据え置かれる公
算が大きいが、基調物価が2％に到達したと判断されると報じられている。 
日米がともに12 月に利上げすれば、名目の政策金利差は変わらない。為替にとって重要なの
は、その先の方向性の違いである。FF金利が明確に中立水準を超えるFRBの利上げが最終局面に近
づく一方、日銀は中立金利を目指して利上げを続ける。この構図が鮮明になれば、日米の実質金
利差は徐々に縮小に向かおう。逆に、原油高を受けて米国の物価上昇が加速し、FRBが一段の利上
げを迫られる展開となれば、実質金利差の縮小は見込めず、ドル円相場の膠着状態を演出しよ
う。

6.  今後の見通し：年末に向けて緩やかな円高を想定、ただし分岐は大きい 
以上を踏まえると、当面のドル円相場は、日米の金融政策会合と中間選挙という大きなイベン

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
トを控え、当面は現状の狭いレンジでの推移が続くと考えられる。10 月27～28 日のFOMCは据え置
きが市場の大勢だ。FOMCで利上げが見送られ、その後の日銀会合で基調物価2％到達が確認されて
12 月利上げへの地ならしが進めば、円高方向に振れやすい組み合わせとなる。しかし、市場では
FRBも年内利上げを実施するとの見方が強く、ドル円相場を動かす材料とはなりにくい。逆に、
FRBが10 月のFOMCで利上げを決定した場合、ドル円相場は159 円を超える円安進展の可能性が高
まる。 
年末にかけては、①中間選挙で民主党が議会の主導権を握り、②イラン情勢が交渉による解決
に向かい原油価格が落ち着き、③日銀が12 月に利上げを実施して来年以降も利上げ継続の姿勢を
示す、という三つの条件がそろえば、日米の実質金利差は縮小に向かう。その場合は、150 円台半
ばから前半への円高が進む可能性がある。投機筋の売建玉が再び積み上がっている局面であれ
ば、その巻き戻しが円高の勢いを増幅することも考えられる。 
一方で、①共和党の善戦、②選挙後のイランへの軍事行動の再開、③FRBの利上げ継続＋日銀の
利上げ慎重姿勢打ち出し、といった組み合わせとなれば、ドル円は再び160 円台を試すことにな
ろう。その場合、政府・日銀は過去の介入水準である160 円台前半から164 円近辺を意識しつ
つ、円安のスピードを抑えるための介入に踏み切る公算が大きい。ただし、これまでの経験が示
すように、ファンダメンタルズが変わらない中での介入は"時間を買う"政策にすぎず、効果が一
時的にとどまる可能性には注意が必要だ。 
筆者は、①米中間選挙は最低でも上下院のねじれ、場合によっては上院においても民主党の過
半数獲得、②選挙後に米国がイランへの軍事行動を再開、同時に仲介国を介した交渉を再開する
ものの、膠着状態が続く、③日米とも利上げ継続、をメインシナリオとしている。 
さらにリスク要因として、①米中間選挙の結果がなかなか確定せず、議会勢力の確定に時間が
かかる、②イラン情勢はフーシ派から攻撃を受けているサウジアラビアや、総選挙を終えたイス
ラエルによる本格的な攻撃参戦、の可能性を挙げる。 
このように不透明要因が多いことも足元のドル円相場の膠着の要因になっていると考えられる
が、短期的な値動きに一喜一憂するよりも、実質金利差という基本的な決定要因がどちらに動い
ているかを冷静に見極めることが重要であり、日米中央銀行の金融政策姿勢が最も注目される。

以 上

2308 台達電(Delta)

報告日期： 2026/11/02 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 營收 1970.6 億 ， 毛利率34.1%， 營利率16.6%，EPS 9.04
元。受到供應鏈缺料以及拉貨遞延影響， 3Q26 毛利率不如預
期。  
 預估 4Q26 營收將維持成長趨勢，毛利率部分，隨著隨著庫存
預計開始去化，以及部分產品漲價部分產品為反映原物料成本
而調漲價格，毛利率將有望回升。  
 HVDC 部分， ±400V 已在下半年開始出貨， 800V 系統亦持續
發展推進，同時液冷、電源機櫃、 DC-DC 模組等新產品線持續
放量；公司亦持續推進固態氧化物燃料電池 (SOFC) ，目前已有
海外大型客戶接觸送樣，初期產能逾 100MW ，預計 2027 年正
式量產。

一、受缺料影響， 3Q26 毛利率 34.1% ，EPS 9.04 元 
台達電 3Q26 營收 1970.6 億， QoQ +7.5% ，YoY +31.1% ，略低
於公司預期，主要由於供應鏈缺料以及資料中心建置延遲，導致
部分貨品出貨遞延 。 就產品組合來看 ，3Q26 電源及零部件 (Power 
Electronics) 佔整體營收的 54% ，交通 (Mobility) 佔 5% ，自動化
(Automation) 佔 8% 基礎建設 (Infrastructure) 佔 33% 。而獲利方
面， 3Q26 營業毛利 672 億， QoQ +2.8% ，YoY +28.1% ，毛利率
34.1% ，QoQ -1.6ppts ，YoY -0.8ppts ，公司表示，第三季存貨增
加，認列約 39 億元的備抵存貨跌價損失。主要由於現有產品缺
料，以及新產品客戶新平台量產較慢、拉貨延後所致，預計第四
季至明年第一季陸續消化並回沖。 3Q26 營業淨利 326.2 億，營利
率 16.6% ，業外淨利 10.46 億，稅後淨利 234.95 億，單季稅後
EPS 9.04 元。

二、庫存去化、部分產品漲價， 4Q26 營運有望回升  
展望 4Q26 ，公司表示，第四季營收確定將維持成長趨勢，而毛利
率部分，公司亦預估，第四季毛利率將優於第三季，主要由於隨
著庫存預計從第四季開始去化，並在明年第一季陸續消化完畢，
而存貨順利出貨後即可回沖。另外，第四季部分產品為反映原物
料成本而調漲價格 。 預估4Q26 營收 2181 億，QoQ +10.7% ，YoY 
+35.0% ，毛利率 35.5% 。營利率 18.2% ，稅後淨利 283 億，單季
稅後 EPS 10.89 元。

三. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 37.53/62.49 元 
展望 2026 年，公司表示， 2026 年全年整體資料中心相關營收佔
比將接近 5 成，其中， AI 電源解決方案營收佔比有望達 25% ，液
冷散熱則大約佔 13% 。預估 2026 全年營收 7574 億元， YoY 
+36.5% ，毛利率 35.5% ，營利率 17.3% ，稅後淨利 974.8 億，全
年稅後 EPS 37.53 元。展望 2027 年，受惠於 CSP 持續擴大資本
支出， AI 資料中心建置需求延續，公司預期， AI 相關營收與液冷
系統產品 2027 年的營收佔比將進一步攀升。 HVDC 部分， ±400V
已在下半年開始出貨 ， 由NVIDIA 推動的 800V 系統亦持續發展推
進，同時液冷、電源機櫃、 DC-DC 模組等新產品線持續放量，此
外，公司亦持續推進固態變壓器 (SST) 與固態氧化物燃料電池
(SOFC) ，初期產能逾 100MW ，預計 2027 年正式量產，目前已有
海外大型客戶接觸送樣。而產能部分，公司亦宣布，將於馬來西
亞檳城設立大型生產據點，預計五年內產能追上泰國廠區。另外，
針對 Vicor 提出侵權訴訟以及美國 ITC 啟動調查，公司則表示，
公司自身擁有多項 IP，且 ITC 程序冗長，短期內對公司出貨與客
戶不會造成影響。預估 2027 全年營收 1 兆 695 億元， YoY 
+41.2% ，毛利率 36.5% ，營利率 20.0% ，稅後淨利 1623 億，全
年稅後 EPS 62.49 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 1680 元 
考量到 CSP 持續擴大資本支出， AI 資料中心建置需求延續，公司
具備關鍵產業地位，且漲價效益及遞延訂單後續將陸續反映，將
有望持續挹注公司營收及獲利成長動能。以公司 2027 年 EPS 來
看，目前股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 21~22 倍本益比，評價仍具
上修空間，給於買進投資建議，目標價 1680 元(27x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
台達電 (Delta) 成立於 1971 年，以製造繞線式磁性元件起家，早期
專注於筆電 (NB) 、桌機 (DT) 、螢幕 (Monitor) 、伺服器 (Server) 、印
表機 (Printer) 及網通等 IT 相關產品之電源供應器與散熱風扇，憑
藉深厚的核心技術延伸發展，穩居全球磁性與散熱元件重要供應
商及全球最大交換式電源供應器龍頭。因應全球電子產業轉變與
環保節能意識抬頭，台達電近年積極跨足工業自動化、智慧安防
及綠能領域，逐步從關鍵零組件製造商轉型為「整體節能解決方
案提供者」，成為全球電源管理與散熱解決方案的專業領導廠商。
目前集團深耕電源及零組件、自動化與基礎設施等核心業務，並
進一步拓展至交通領域，依 2025 年產品結構比重來看，分別為電

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

2. 液冷散熱成高階 AI 基礎設施標配，估 2026 年滲透率達 53% 
根據 TrendForce 最新研究顯示，隨著 AI 基礎設施建置浪潮及高
階晶片運算功率不斷攀升，單晶片熱設計功耗已超越 1kW ，使液

AMD 與 Google 等巨頭的高階晶片與整櫃式方案強力推動下，預
估 2026 年液冷散熱於 AI 晶片的滲透率將從 2025 年的 33% 大幅
躍升至 53%，並在 2027 年進一步逼近 60%。其中， Google 因積
極導入自研 TPU 並率先全面推廣整櫃式液冷設計，成為主要推手
之一。此外，隨著 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 平台全面導入全液冷架構，
水冷板、分歧管及冷卻分配系統等關鍵零組件需求隨之大增，帶
動相關供應鏈如 AVC、Cooler Master 等業者積極搶攻各大 CSP
與 AI 平台商機。

圖三、 2023-2027 年液冷散熱於 AI 晶片滲透率預估

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

3. 預估 2026 年全球資料中心用電需求年增 31% 
根據  TrendForce 產業研究顯示 ， 受惠於AI 應用需求激增與雲端
服務供應商 (CSP) 加速擴建基礎設施 ， 全球資料中心電力需求容量
預計將由 2025 年的 122.9GW 攀升至 2026 年的 161GW ，YoY 
+31% ，並於 2027 年維持超過 30% 的高成長率；其中， AI 伺服器
為核心增長動能，占整體電力需求比重由 2025 年的 25% 提升至
2026 年的 33.4% ，預計 2027 年更將突破 40% ，同步帶動散熱與
供電等非 IT 設備需求，促使資料中心規劃導入高壓直流供電
(HVDC) 以優化供配電效率。然而，電網供電增速卻未能跟上需求
擴張 ， 全球資料中心電力供需自2026 年起開始背離 ， 並將於2028
年後因電網併網與輸配電建設交付遞延而顯著擴大；預估至 2030
年，全球資料中心電力需求將達 490.7GW ，但電網可供容量僅
222.6GW ，供需缺口高達 268GW( 其中資料中心部署量最大的美
國自 2028 年起遞延加劇，至 2030 年缺口預計超過 170GW) ，未
來此一實質短缺與帳面落差能否透過業者自建發電及電網改善來
填補，仍有待觀察。

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Note: META, AMZN and GOOG are covered by

Brian Nowak. MSFT is covered by Adam Wood.
We view US hyperscaler commentary as positive for the Greater China supply
chain: The tone was uniformly positive, with demand still outstripping supply. With
CY26 nearly over, the focus was on CY27 capex. Although quantitative guidance was
not provided this quarter, US hyperscalers’ comments suggested that demand
would remain robust into CY27.
We stay OW on the Greater China supply chain: We like Wistron, a key L6 partner

to Nvidia and AMD, with new customers bringing more L10 (Lenovo and an AI lab)
and L11 AI server exposure (Amazon, Oracle). We also like Lenovo, a key general-
purpose server supplier to Microsoft and Oracle, but also a rising supplier of AI
servers, with ~US$54bn AI server pipeline as of C2Q26/F1Q27. We continue to like
Wiwynn, a key general-purpose server supplier to three hyperscalers (Microsoft,
Meta and Amazon) and a potential assembly partner for two new customers (a

hyperscaler and an AI lab). Wiwynn is also a key supplier of Trainium server racks, as
well as AMD Helios racks for Meta.  We like GCE and EMC/TUC as beneficiaries of
US hyperscalers’ demand for PCB and CCL in general-purpose and AI servers. We
like Unimicron and NYPCB, Greater China’s key ABF substrate suppliers for
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
MSFT lowered its CY26 capex guidance by US$5bn to US$170bn, reflecting
delays in third-party capacity deliveries rather than a demand issue.
MSFT also guided for capex to “accelerate” in C1H27, driven mainly by CPUs
(higher memory & storage costs), GPUs and delayed third-party capacity
deliveries.
META narrowed its CY26 capex guidance to US$135-145bn (from US$130-145bn)

and said it would keep investing “aggressively” into CY27.
GOOG maintained its CY26 capex guidance of US$195-205bn and reiterated that
CY27 capex will increase “significantly” y/y.
AMZN maintained its CY26 capex guidance of US$220bn and said CY27 capex
would be “meaningfully” higher than CY26.
November 2, 2026 02:07 AM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 180: 261105_ubs_elan

### Page 1

ab 5 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Elan Microelectronics
Q426E likely to be the near-term trough
Q326 earnings below on softer NB demand environment
Elan's Q326 EPS of NT$0.58 (net profit NT$168mn) was below UBSe/street of NT
$1.29/1.71, as a result of lower revenue amid weaker NB demand environment. Q326
revenue was already reported at NT$2.2bn (-31% QoQ), below UBSe/street of NT
$2.4/2.8 bn as NB brand customers further reduced their orders from late-Aug on softer

end-demand after retail price hike and higher component inventories. GM was down
2.7ppts QoQ to 44.7% on less favourable mix, directionally in-line with UBSe of ~2ppt
QoQ decline. It booked non-op loss of NT$100mn, vs NT$294mn gain in Q226, mainly
due to unrealized loss on financial assets. Despite softer earnings, Elan remains
committed on its high dividend yield policy and is considering to raise its payout.
Near-term demand remains muted but Q426E likely to be the trough

Elan is restricted from providing Q4 financial guidance given the accumulated non-op
gain in past four quarters exceeded 10% of the net profit. With October sales reported
at NT$461 mn (-23% MoM), Elan expects Q426 remains a challenging quarter amid
softer demand environment and ongoing inventory adjustment. Nevertheless, we think
channel inventory is on track to be depleted in the coming months and Q426 is likely to
mark the trough for near-term revenue. We forecast Q426E sales to decline 38% QoQ in

Q426E but will rebound by 27% QoQ in Q127E on restocking. Into 2027, Elan noted it
has won some new projects at top NB brands, while also sees share gain opportunities
following its peer's business restructure, as well as ramping low-cost solution to capture
market trend. It also see good progress of haptics (2026 sales 5-6x vs 2025) and single
layer on-cell (3-4x), and we believe the momentum should continue into 2027.
ADAS and Drone remain key long-term growth drivers

Elan's AI-related revenue reached 14% of sales in Q326 and is expected to further
increase in Q426F as AI revenue remains more resilient vs NB-related products. Its ADAS
revenue is primarily driven by EV bus now and it thinks the proliferation into logistic
vehicles should be the next driver. Its drone-related business is on track to see higher
revenue contribution from 2027F as it has penetrated into Thunder Tiger's supply chain,
as well as two other customers, supplying both domestic and overseas demand. Elan

noted on top of the camera modules, its obstacle avoidance system, which carries much
higher ASP of US$800, could start to ramp from Q127F. We forecast its AI-business to
see a 45% sales CAGR in 2025-30E, outpacing traditional HMI.
Valuation: Maintain buy with a NT$150 price target
We cut 2026/27/28E EPS to NT$6.35/6.94/11.55 from NT$8.19/10.82/13.28 on softer
NB demand and prolonged inventory adjustment. We lower our PT to NT$150 (from NT
$170) using a lower multiple of 15x H227-H128E PE (from 16x 2027E P/E) to factor in

overall market valuation contraction. We view current headwinds as cyclical and believe
the market underappreciates Elan's AI opportunities and   upside from share gain.

Company Description
Elan Microelectronics is a Taiwan-based fabless IC design house specialising in human-
machine interface including touchscreen controllers, touchpad modules, pointing stick
modules and fingerprint sensors, specifically for notebook PCs. It started with
microcontrollers (MCUs) for PC peripherals and consumer electronics upon establishment in

1994, and further expanded the reach into notebook PC input devices through the
acquisition of a related subsidiary under KOA Corporation in 2003.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a target PE multiple.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights.

For Elan, we believe key risks include: 1) PC and NB demand dynamics, with any slowdown in
these end-markets directly impacting Elan's growth and also its new product roll-outs; 2) a
longer-than-expected product qualification cycle or slower-than-expected product adoption
among clients; 3) fiercer-than-expected price competition from global and Asian peers
negatively impacting growth and gross margin; and 5) faster-than-expected foundry and
assembly cost increases negatively impacting gross margin.

### Page 5

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over the next 12
months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA)  is the one-year local
market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR) Stocks may be flagged
by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

## Document 228: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업.pdf

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

KOSPI 5937.66

## Document 354: Increasing signs of a bottom

Source PDF: all report pdf/Increasing signs of a bottom.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Weekly Market Views)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office 
6 November 2026

Increasing signs  
of a bottom

 Democrats’ thumping win in the US 
mid-term elections has three key 
implications: i) policy gridlock is back in 
Washington, likely stalling fiscal 
expansion and new tariffs; ii) the 
chance of a prolonged Middle East 
conflict is curtailed, lowering oil prices 
and inflation expectations; and iii) the 
pace of AI data-centre buildout is at 
risk, curbing AI-linked bond supply. 
 For portfolios, the election reinforces 
our mid-cycle playbook: remaining 
broadly balanced across assets, while 
looking for tactical opportunities.  
 As the S&P500 volatility index (VIX) 
surpasses 25, we see rising signs of 
equity market bottoming. We add an 
opportunistic idea on the US IT sector, 
while using volatility to earn income 
through structured strategies.   
 In bonds, we prefer the 3-7-year part 
of the Developed Market bond yield 
curve and BBB-to-BB-rated corporate 
bonds to earn attractive income as AI 
supply eases. In FX, we position for a 
modestly weaker USD over 6-12 
months via our allocation to ex-US 
assets.

What are the equity market 
implications of the US mid-
term elections? 
Does emerging US jobs data 
alter your view on Fed rates 
and US bond yields? 
What is your outlook for GBP 
after this week’s BoE meeting?

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

before and after the mid-term elections

Editorial 
Increasing signs of a bottom 
Strategy summary: Democrats’ thumping win in the US mid -
term elections has three key implications: i) policy gridlock is 
back in Washington, likely stalling fiscal expansion and new 
tariffs; ii) the chance of a prolonged Middle East conflict is 
curtailed, lowering oil prices and inflation expectations; and  iii) 
the pace of AI data-centre buildout is at risk , curbing AI-linked 
bond supply. Higher corporate and wealth taxes are a long-term 
risk if Democrats win the 2028 presidential elections.   
For portfolios, the election reinforce s our mid-cycle playbook 
already in place:  remaining broadly balanced across assets, 
while looking for tactical opportunities. As the S&P500 volatility 
index (VIX) surpasses 25, we see rising signs of equity market 
bottoming. We add an opportunistic idea on the US IT sector 
(see page 4), while using elevated volatility to earn income 
through structured strategies. In bonds, we prefer the 3-7-year 
part of the Developed Market bond yield curve and BBB-to-BB-
rated corporate bonds to earn attractive income as AI supply 
eases. In FX, we position for a modestly weaker USD over 6 -
12 months by allocating to ex-US assets.  
Policy implications:  Oil fell 12% over the past week as 
Democrats captured the US Senate and House. The party also 
flipped governorships in five states – Georgia, Iowa, Nevada, 
Ohio and Texas – securing a majority of governorships among 
50 US states for the first time since 2010. Vice President Vance 
and Republican leaders attributed the defeat to the Iran conflict, 
making it harder for President Trump to sustain it. Democrat 
control of the defence budget from next year further constrains 
conflict escalation. Despite near-term escalation risks, we see 
the geopolitical risk premium in oil prices fading, with oil easing 
towards USD 80/bbl  over 6-12 months. Oil-driven disinflation 
should allow central banks to adopt less aggressive policies. 
Adding tactical exposure to US IT sector : Semiconductors 
slumped this week amid concerns over data-centre moratoria, 
while beneficiaries of Democrat policies include defensives, 
affordability-related stocks, tariff rollback beneficiaries and  the 
healthcare sector. We use the volatility to add tactical exposure 
to the US IT sector . As we enter mid -cycle dynamics, equities 
should return 8-10% annually. The 2015–2017 mid-cycle offers 
a useful comparison: with an opposition Congress,  S&P 500 
returns slowed to 8.3% , but remained positive. Diversification 
across sectors and regions remains a priority amid the rotation. 
Prefer belly of the bond curve: Government bonds rallied on 
expectations of slower growth. We still expect two more Fed 
hikes by H1 2027, given sticky near-term inflation and a robust 
economy. However, cheaper oil should lower inflation towards 
the Fed’s 2% target next year, allowing rate cuts in H2 2027. 
Ultra-long yields remain elevated due to  fiscal challenges. 
Bonds, particularly those from non-hyperscaler investment -
grade corporates , offer attractive income. The 3 -7-year 
segment – the belly of the yield curve – offers the best balance 
of income and sensitivity to interest-rate changes. 
Credit to benefit from restrained AI supply:  Since states  
control data centre regulations, Democrat wins in governorship 
races in leading data centre states, such as Texas and Georgia, 
means growth in this segment is likely to be restricted. Slower 
data-centre construction could improve credit supply -demand 
conditions due to reduced issuance by hyperscalers. However, 
some data -centre-focused deals could become less viable, 
warranting caution towards lower -quality issuers. High -yield 
credit spreads have widened, but BB- and B-rated bonds have 
held up, while CCC -rated debt has r eached distressed levels. 
We prefer the BBB–BB range, spanning the boundary between 
investment-grade and high-yield debt, for income. 
A modest headwind for the USD, tailwind for gold: The 
dollar should remain range-bound near term before weakening 
modestly over 6-12 months. Democrat control of the Congress 
limits tariff escalation , fiscal expansion and rapid AI buildout , 
three pillars of US exceptionalism supporting the USD through 
2026. Disinflation should also encourage a less aggressive Fed 
stance, removing another source of USD support. Near -term 
support for the USD index (DXY) remains at 100.7 . A weaker-
than-expected payrolls report tonight could accelerate the 
USD’s decline, and vice versa . A softer dollar and sustained 
central-bank buying, led by China, remain supportive of gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the equity market implications of the US mid -
term election results?   
Our view: The US mid -term election outcome adds political 
headwinds but does not alter our outlook for positive equity 
returns. Investors should maintain a Core allocation to global 
equities and continue to diversify their equity exposure across 
regions and sectors. We initiate an opportunistic idea on the US 
information technology (IT) sector, moving our opportunistic 
idea positioning from a de fensive tilt to a more balanced 
positioning. Higher volatility also presents opportunities to use 
structured strategies for equity exposure.  
Rationale: The Democrats’ victory in both chambers of Congress 
has supported the outperformance of themes aligned with their 
policy priorities , with clean energy stocks , including solar 
companies, among the notable beneficiaries. Affordability-related 
themes have also benefited, including companies that could gain 
from potential tariff rollbacks. Medicaid-related stocks  have also 
risen on hopes that healthcare will gain from policy changes. 
Meanwhile, semiconductor stocks remain under pressure  amid 
concerns about potential moratoriums on data centres. Our AI capital 
expenditure (capex) estimates remain conservative relative to 
consensus, but with a potential reset in expectations around data 
centre spending, investors should remain selective.  
We initiate an opportunistic idea on the US IT sector after a 
significant 17% correction this quarter. Historically, this has been 
followed by strong positive returns over the next few quarters ( see 
table). Semiconductors, about 40% of US IT, appears oversold after 
correcting more than 30% since we closed our opportunistic idea a 
month ago. Earnings growth for US IT continues to be solid, with 
volume driven growth in semiconductors, AI monetisation 
opportunities, ongoing cloud growth and resilient services revenue.  
As we highlighted in our November 2026 Global Market Outlook , 
the transition to mid -cycle is typically characterised by greater 
political headwinds. The mid -term election outcome reinforces this 
trend. The mid-cycle experience of 2015-17 offers a useful historical 
parallel for returns that moderate, but remain positive. Although we 
trimmed global equities to a Core allocation last week, we continue 
to expect equities to deliver mid-to-high single-digit returns per 
annum during the mid -cycle, even amid political headwinds, 
supported by steady earnings growth in both 2027 and 2028. 
Stay invested and diversify, with Overweight allocations to the US 
and A xJ, which offer the strongest earnings growth. Sector 
diversification is key as we expect market leadership to broaden. We 
are Overweight the US IT, US healthcare, US financial, US materials, 
Europe ex-UK financial and Japan financial sectors. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation 
cycle is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle 
transition, where equity returns moderate but 
remain positive

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are the key takeaways from Q3 2026 US earnings? 
Our view:  Q3 US earnings remain robust, but more modest 
earnings beats reinforce our view that equity markets are 
entering a mid-cycle phase. We remain Overweight US equities, 
but recommend investors to maintain diversified sector 
exposure as returns moderate and market leadership broadens. 
Rationale: As of 30 October, 82% of S&P500 companies that ha d 
reported had beaten earnings estimates. However, aggregate 
earnings were just 4.5% above expectations, a smaller beat than in 
recent quarters. Still, Q3 US earnings growth remain s robust at 
31.6% y/y, supporting expected full-year growth of 36.1% in 2026 
and 16.4% in 2027 for the S&P500. The slower pace of upgrades 
suggests the bull market is maturing  as investors focus on the 
sustainability of earnings growth. This raises the hurdle for positive 
share price reactions and makes selectivity more essential. For Big 
Tech, capex commitments remain high, while free cash flow is under 
pressure and expected to remain negative. Financing risks weigh on 
sentiment, particularly for companies with weaker balance sheets.  
We remain Overweight US equities , given the strong earnings 
growth, but we expect elevated volatility  and modest returns. We 
prefer quality exposure within technology  while maintaining  
diversified exposure across US financials, healthcare and materials. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  What to expect from Japanese banks’ upcoming results?   
Our view:  We expect  Japanese banks’  earnings growth  to 
continue over the medium term, supported by higher domestic 
net interest income (NII) and resilient fee income. We remain 
positive on the sector, favouring banks with strong deposit 
franchises and scope to increase shareholder returns. 
Investors will focus on whether full -year guidance upgrades 
and buybacks exceed expectations. 
Rationale: The BoJ’s September rate hike to 1.25% took effect late 
in the quarter, so its benefit should be more visible from the 
December quarter as loans reprice. We expect three further BoJ 
rate hikes by June 2027, supporting further NII expansion.  We 
will also look for signs that stronger corporate investment is driving 
faster loan growth and higher financing and advisory fees.  
We prefer to place more weight on guidance upgrades driven by 
recurring lending and fee income than on those driven by gains on 
equity disposals. Continued reductions in cross -shareholdings 
could free up capital for additional buybacks, providing a further 
boost to return on equity  (ROE). Key risks:  faster-than-expected 
deposit rate repricing, rising credit costs and bond portfolio losses. 
—  Robin Xie, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are the fundamental reasons behind your India 
equity upgrade? Which sectors offer favourable risk-reward? 
Our View: Indian equities offer an increasingly attractive risk -
reward profile. With India’s valuation premium to Emerging 
Markets (EMs) narrowing, foreign ownership near decade lows, 
Underweight EM positioning  and a stabilising Indian rupee 
(INR), conditions are increasingly supportive of Indian equity 
outperformance vs. the broader AxJ region.  
Rationale: We have upgraded Indian equities to Overweight within 
our AxJ equities allocation. While Indian markets  have witnessed 
USD 32bn of foreign investor outflows in 2026 year-to-date, strong 
domestic institutional investor inflows of USD 74bn  have more 
than offset foreign selling. These outflows and the Indian equity 
market’s underperformance vs . global peers reflect broader EM 
allocation shifts towards AI , rather than weakening India n 
fundamentals. Capital has gravitated toward s markets such as the 
US, Taiwan and  South Korea, which offer direct exposure to AI 
beneficiaries. However, we expect market leadership to broaden, as 
the rapid AI buildout is increasing ly debt-funded, and US mid-term 
election results raise uncertainty. We prefer to diversify and we see 
investors reallocating towards structurally strong  growth markets 
such as India, which offer non-AI driven growth.  
The RBI’s measures have supported INR stability,  but underlying 
USD demand remains elevated. Lower oil prices would improve 
external balances, but the INR is likely to follow a path of gradual, 
controlled depreciation rather than a quick downward move. On 
valuation to its own historical average, the Nifty 50 one-year forward 
P/E is trading 18% below its long-term average while ROE at 14.7% 
is above historical averages. With India now marginally Underweight 
in large EM portfolios compared to  the c.2% average Overweight 
historically, the case for a recovery in foreign flows is 
strengthening. 
India’s earnings growth remains intact . The Nifty 50 clocked Q1 
FY27 and Q2 FY27 profit-after-tax growth of c.20% y/y (ex -oil 
marketing companies) . Large -caps offer greater earnings visibility 
and downside protection, while stronger growth in small - and mid-
caps warrants a selective approach given elevated valuations.  
India’s growth outlook remains supported by a strengthening 
investment cycle, manufacturing momentum, supply chain 
diversification, resilient consumption and healthy corporate and 
banking balance sheets. Importantly, earnings growth remains 
robust and broa d-based across the financial, metals, telecom, 
automobiles and other domestic sectors, with Nifty 50 earnings 
growth expectations of c.14-15% over 2027 vis a vis A xJ’s 
technology driven earnings growth of 29% in 2027. 
—  Shaily Gang, Chief Investment Strategist, India

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Does the emerging US job market data alter your view of 
the Fed’s rate path and government bond yields? 
Our view: We do not expect the recent and incoming US job 
market data to alter our outlook unless there are significant 
deviations from the US economy’s current trajectory.  
Rationale: The US economy continues to exhibit resilience, with the 
latest Job Openings and Labour Turnover Survey data showing job 
openings surprising to the upside at 7.5mn. Initial jobless claims 
remain near three-month lows. There is little evidence of a material 
slowdown in labour demand. Inflation is expected to trend lower 
through H2 2027, but the disinflationary impulse remains a medium-
term story and is unlikely to manifest meaningfully in the near term. 
The market is now pricing in two additional 25bps hikes by H1 
2027 (down from three previously), in line with our view. These two 
rate hikes are largely priced into the US government bond yield 
curve. We estimate 2- and 10-year US government bond yields to 
range between 4.5-4.75% and 5-5.25%, respectively, over the next 
three months . Our twelve -month forecast for 2- and 10 -year US 
government bond yields are 4.25-4.5% and 4.75-5%, respectively, 
The 2-year yield is already trading within our expected range and 
would only move materially higher if a significantly stronger -than-
expected payrolls print led markets to price in a third hike. With two 
hikes baked in, long -run disinflation expectations intact and an 
uncertain fiscal outlook, the longer end of the  yield curve is 
expected to be less sensitive to a single payrolls release. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is your outlook for the GBP following the BoE’s rate 
hike this week? Do you see any impact on EUR/GBP? 
Our view: The BoE  rate hike supports  the GBP, but upside 
remains limited. We remain constructive on EUR/GBP, 
expecting a rebound towards 0.8450. 
Rationale: The BoE hiked by 25bps to 4% as expected . The GBP 
was little changed, despite hawkish inflation guidance. With markets 
pricing only around 50bps of additional BoE tightening over the next 
12 months, we see the GBP remaining vulnerable. In contrast, the 
ECB held its deposit rate at 2.50% in October but signalled readiness 
to tighten if inflation remains elevated. We expect Euro area inflation 
to rise further, keeping the ECB’s tightening bias alive. This suggests 
EUR/GBP has fallen too far relative to interest rate differentials.  
Germany’s planned increase in federal debt issuance  should 
support Euro area growth, while Moody’s decision not to downgrade 
France has removed an important EUR tail risk. Meanwhile, tighter 
UK fiscal policy is likely to weigh on domestic growth and business 
investment. We expect EUR/GBP to recover towards 0.8450. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

### Page 8

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is the AUD underperforming despite the RBA hike? 
Our view: The AUD has lagged because markets had largely 
priced in the RBA’s November rate hike to 4.85%, and the bank 
gave investors less reason to expect another. Equity weakness 
also weighed on the currency. We remain bullish on AUD/USD  
and would add on pullbacks. Australia’s interest rate advantage 
and a sustained recovery in risk sentiment should support a 
move towards 0.72 in three months and 0.74 in 12 months. 
Rationale: Ahead of the 3 November RBA rate decision, market 
pricing implied an 88% chance of a 25bp s hike, leaving little scope 
for an upside surprise. What changed after the announcement 
was the pricing of the next hike.  The RBA dropped its reference 
to further rate increases, and three members preferred a rate hold. 
Markets scaled back the odds of rates reaching 5.10% next year to 
around 30%, thus weighing on the AUD. 
The AUD is sensitive to swings in global risk appetite, especially 
the US tech sell-off spread to global equities, triggering margin calls 
and weighing on  the currency. An equity recovery would ease that 
pressure. Even after the two 25bp s Fed hikes we expect by mid -
2027, Australia would retain a 35bps rate advantage  over the top 
of the Fed’s target range, supporting a gradual AUD recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

We see AUD/USD upside risks on a sustained 
recovery in risk sentiment

► 伊朗支持的葉門胡塞武裝組織持續攻擊沙烏
地阿 拉 伯 和荷 姆 茲 海峽 船 隻，美 期 中 選舉
前，地緣政治風險不減 ，添股市賣壓 。中國
長鑫存儲在新型晶片研發取得進展 ，衝擊半
導體類股 ，美光與三星 、海力士記憶體廠即
使仍供不應求 ，亦遭市場拋售 ，南韓股指週
跌幅逾 18% 。 網 通 晶 片 大 廠 博 通 執 行 長 表

示：資金是 AI建設擴張面臨的最大瓶頸 ，光
通訊股領跌走弱，科技、半導體股重挫。
► 美股第三季財報登場 ，花旗、高盛本季淨利
優於預期 ，但富國銀行淨利息收入失色 ，摩
根大通自 2023年4Q以來 ，首次獲利不如預
期，美銀亦不佳 。艾斯摩爾上修全年營收展
望，透露部分客戶已洽詢2029年至2030年設
備訂單，顯示晶片擴產需求仍強 。台積電3Q
毛利率達66.4%較前月略減，因2奈米快速放
量與海外廠產能利用率造成稀釋 ，但今年資
本支出財測維持600到640億美元不變，反映
代理AI客戶需求強勁 ，預期2026年至2028年
資本支出將較過去三年進一步顯著提高。

► 美國 9 月CPI 、PPI 皆上升 ， 且 超 過 市 場 預
期，9月PPI月增率為 1.3%，創2022年來最
大漲幅，高於市場預期的月增 0.5%，年增率
達6.3%，為2022年12月來最大漲幅 ，顯示
通膨壓力正從能源擴大至其他領域 。通膨僵
固性使市場預期高利率將維持更久時間 ，美
國公債殖利率一度走高 ，但隨股市重挫 、避
險資金轉入 ，公債止跌回升 。體質較弱的企
業債出現警訊 ，CoreWeave債券殖利率大幅
攀升 ， 2032 年 到 期 債 券 殖 利 率 一 度 升 至
15%，反應公司舉債擴建資料中心 ，市場擔
憂其債 務規 模 、 利 息負擔 及未來 再融 資能
力，除違約風險上升 ，過高成本侵蝕企業獲
利、股債皆挫 ，顯示目前即使是 AI、科技信
用債表現也開始出現分化。
► 中東地緣風險推升油價上漲 ，儘管川普宣布

將暫時放寬對俄羅斯柴油出口的限制 ，西德
州油價仍處於每桶 98美元高位 ，推升全球通
膨預期 。虛擬資產比特幣 、以太幣隨風險性
資產同步下挫。

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至 3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由 2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率 0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因 ，
貢獻整體 CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導 ，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性 ，需求面
通膨略有提高 ，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在 4%以上維持更久 。受
到油價高盤 、通膨僵固 ，與財政惡化 、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響 ，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中 ， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈 ，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大 、
歐元走弱 ，美元轉強 ，商品、黃金承壓 。法
國政治 不確定性讓 歐股修正 、法股最疲 ，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高 ，增加企業借貸成本

與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，Goldman Sachs，凱基證券整理，註：右上圖之大型雲端服務供應商(CSP)企業為Amazon、Alphabet、Microsoft、Oracle
► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐
熱門議題
美股第三季獲利成長持續上修，科技為主要支撐 CSP雲端營收加速，資本支出變現跡象持續受驗證
評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張
熱門議題財報展望

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

### Page 8

內部參考文件請勿外流
► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

投資焦點
近3個月漲幅和預估獲利呈正成長，醫療生技股佳
醫療保健和生技預估獲利明年明顯成長，有利股價續揚
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基投顧，凱基證券整理
預估今明年產業獲利差異，醫療保健和生技成長最大

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 366: LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI  investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

LatAm’s silicon dividend: 
tailwinds from the AI  
investment boom in the U.S.

1 
Authors 
Fancisco Campos 
Chief LatAm Economist

Beatriz Garcia Nunes 
LatAm Economist

October 28, 2026 
Key messages: 
- U.S. investment has broken away from Europe and China since 2023, 
powered by AI-related equipment, software and research spending. 
- The AI CapEx boom demands commodities and merchandise that LatAm 
can supply.  
- Latin America’s export gains are real but concentrated. Copper, computer 
and electronics products provide the clearest links to the U.S. AI buildout. 
- Chile is the region’s most direct copper beneficiary. Mexico captures the 
windfall stemming from increases hardware demand and trade-diversion. In 
these cases, the AI-related tailwinds have undergirded appreciable 
improvements in external accounts. 
- The cases for nickel and zinc illustrate that the dividend is not widespread. 
U.S. imports of the former have mostly returned toward pre-pandemic levels, 
while for the latter bilateral gains partly reflect supplier substitution. 
- While expanding AI use, supply shortages and incentives can extend the 
CapEx boom, its financial viability poses downside risks. 
Another ‘American exceptionalism’ story  
Investment has become one of the sharpest dividing lines among the world’s 
core economic blocs. Since 2024, U.S. gross fixed capital formation has firmed 
up appreciably. Meanwhile, investment in Europe has broadly stalled while in 
China’s it has retreated, the drivers of which are beyond the scope of this report 
(Figure 1).  
The U.S. divergence has largely been an AI story: a burst of spending on 
computing power, digital infrastructure and intellectual property has offset 
weakness in conventional construction and housing. Indeed, total U.S. fixed 
investment is nearly 12% above its 2023 level. Within it, investment in 
information-processing equipment has risen by roughly 57%, software outlays by 
about 35%, and research and development by around 15%. By contrast, non-
residential structures and residential investment sit below their 2023 levels 
(Figure 2).

### Page 2

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 2

Figure 1: The global investment divergence

Figure 2: AI-related categories lead U.S. investment

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Eurostat, National Bureau of Statistics of 
China, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Latin America’s commodity channel 
AI is not merely a semiconductor story. Chips require specialized minerals; data 
centers require electricity and cooling; electricity requires grids, transformers and 
storage. That chain pulls in copper, aluminum, silver, tin, lithium and selected 
semiconductor inputs, along with natural gas and uranium. Latin America has 
meaningful exposure to several of these inputs, but copper has emerged as the 
commodity with the cleanest and most economically significant regional link. 
Copper is used throughout the AI infrastructure stack, including power cables, 
substations, transformers, server boards, heat sinks and cooling systems. Rising 
U.S. copper demand has lifted imports from Chile and Peru, particularly the 
former (Figure 3). T he effect matters beyond merchandise export volumes. 
Favorable terms-of-trade have helped Chile generate a current -account surplus 
for the first time in roughly fifteen years (0.2% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -
quarter rolling basis ) and have allowed Peru to log the largest surplus in about 
forty-five years (4.5% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4-quarter rolling basis).

28 October 2026

### Page 3

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Figure 3: Copper epitomized the commodity link

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Not every metal is copper 
The broader mining story demands more caution, though. U.S. nickel imports have 
recovered toward pre-Covid levels, but the upswing has not displayed the same 
persistence as copper. Mexico has captured a disproportionate share of the 
bilateral improvement, yet that gain looks more like normalization than a 
sustained AI -driven break (Figure 4). Zinc tells a similar story. U.S. real imports 
stand shy of their pre-pandemic levels, and stronger purchases from Mexico and 
Peru partly reflect a reallocation away from Brazil rather than a decisive increase 
in U.S. demand (Figure 5).

Figure 4: Nickel: recovery, not a boom

Figure 5: Zinc: a substitution story

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

28 October 2026

### Page 4

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

Mexico’s hardware advantage 
The larger non-commodity prize is in computers and electronics. U.S. imports of 
these products have sky -rocketed, while the supplier mix has shifted away from 
China. Mexico and various Asian economies have gained share on the back of this 
double-whammy impulse (Figure 6). 
This helps explain why Mexico’s non-automotive manufacturing exports, notably 
electronics, have outpaced automotive shipments to the U.S. and pressures from 
higher oil prices. The resulting non -oil trade surplus has offset pressure from the 
oil balance and helped Mexico generate a balanced current account (0% of GDP 
as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -quarter rolling basis). The external boost is meaningful, 
although it sho uld not necessarily be interpreted as bona fide  evidence of 
nearshoring, in our view.

Figure 6: U.S. computer & electronics imports going into overdrive

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research.

How much runway remains? 
Quantifying or mapping out a timeframe for the AI investment outlook is beyond 
the scope of this piece or our expertise. Nevertheless, we’d venture to argue that 
the capex cycle appears more likely to mature gradually than to stop abruptly . 
One reason is that the investment case is shifting from frontier -model training 
toward enterprise inference . Capacity that once served development can 
increasingly run commercial workloads for companies. That creates a path to 
revenue even if artificial general intelligence (AGI) crystallizes later than 
expected. It also spreads demand to a much larger corporate customer base.  
A second reason is scarcity. Graphics processing units (GPUs) remain tight, while 
power availability, grid connections, cooling equipment, construction capacity 
and data -center complexity limit how quickly supply can respond. Bottlenecks 
may therefore exte nd the cycle. The constraint is especially relevant for copper 
28 October 2026

### Page 5

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

and power infrastructure, where projects have long lead times and permitting 
hurdles. 
A third reason is strategic. AI development resembles a prisoner’s dilemma at 
both the corporate and national levels. Each participant may worry about costs, 
safety and control, yet slowing unilaterally risks surrendering technological, 
commercial or natio nal security advantages. The same tension is visible in calls 
for guardrails alongside continued spending. Competition can therefore sustain 
capex even when short-term returns are uncertain. 
We acknowledge the risks, nonetheless. Higher borrowing costs raise the hurdle 
rate for an infrastructure buildout financed partly through debt. More important, 
revenues must eventually justify the installed capital base. The bearish case is not 
that AI la cks value; it is that infrastructure spending may run ahead of 
monetization, compromising some companies’ repayment capacity. Public 
opposition to the development of AI infrastructure and/or adoption of AI 
technologies could also slow down the technology’s roll-out. 
The regional balance  
Summing up, the AI investment boom in the U .S. is delivering a dividend to parts 
of Latin America, not the region as a whole. Chile and Peru benefit through copper 
prices and export volumes. Mexico benefits through electronics manufacturing 
and the replacement of Chinese supply. For Brazil and Colombia, the AI dividend 
is rather muted. Where materialized, furthermore, the dividend supports trade 
and current accounts more clearly rather than improving the domestic 
investment. 
These dynamics should persist while U.S. AI investment remains supply -
constrained and strategically protected. If widespread adoption advances and 
the strategic race intensifies, the impulse can last. If financing costs rise further 
or monetization disappo ints, the cycle may narrow. Either way, copper and 
Mexican electronics remain the region’s most visible gauges of the U.S. AI 
investment appetite.

28 October 2026

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
LatAm’s silicon dividend: tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. – October 28, 2026

## Document 391: Policy, politics and earnings

Source PDF: all report pdf/Policy, politics and earnings.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Weekly Market Views)
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office 
23 October 2026

Policy, politics  
and earnings

 We retain our defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, 
sectors and regions amid near-term 
uncertainty around monetary policy,

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Charts of the week: Watching earnings and a potential ‘Blue wave’

US Big Tech earnings, mid-term elections and major central bank policy meetings are the near-term market drivers 
Consensus S&P500 index Q3 earnings estimates by sector   Chance of Democrats winning control of US House and Senate*

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *Polymarket probability

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary:  We retain our near -term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid -term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3 -7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15 -year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US?  Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities  among all sc enarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer -term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fa llible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bond s (CoCos1) 
and US 10 -year go vernment bond ideas:  We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10 -year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:   Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share -price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monet isation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence.  We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight , while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral  and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the key takeaways from the technology sector 
earnings reported so far this week? 
Our view: Recent  technology sector earnings results point to 
strong compute demand and continued capex expansion, 
supporting our positive long -term view on the AI investment 
theme. However, rising funding and execution risks warrant 
near-term caution. We prefer to avoid increasing exposure to 
higher-risk areas within the technology sector for now. 
Rationale: Earnings results reported this week suggest major tech 
companies remain committed to expanding their AI infrastructure. 
While this bodes well for AI infrastructure providers, ambitious 
expansion plans imply that an increasing share of AI capex is likely 
to depend on debt financing. We expect debt-financed AI capex to 
rise from c.25% of AI capex in 2026 to c.40% in 2027.  
Greater reliance on borrowing increases sensitivity to interest rates, 
while deployment delays could postpone revenue recognition amid 
ongoing financing costs. However, evidence of improving AI 
monetisation and adoption should strengthen operating cash 
flows and support internal capex funding. Against this backdrop, 
we will assess Big Tech earnings next week for greater clarity on 
capex guidance and funding and AI monetisation trends.  
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Euro  area bank equities amid 
heightened political and fiscal uncertainty in the region? 
Our view: We remain positive on Euro  area bank equities, 
supported by resilient earnings and shareholder-friendly capital 
return policies. While Fr ance’s fiscal uncertainty and a softer 
growth outlook warrant caution, the recent pullback in Euro  
area bank equities  offers selective opportunities. We favour 
well-capitalised banks with strong deposit franchises and 
diversified earnings. 
Rationale: We expect Euro  area banks’ Q3 2026 net interest 
income to grow 1.5 -3.0% q/q , led by Spanish banks, driven by 
higher rates and continued asset repricing. Resilient fee income from 
wealth management and investment banking activity, as well as 
dividends and buybacks, should provide further support. Price-to-
book (P/B) valuations have eased , despite expectations for 
healthy return on equity (ROE), thus improving risk-reward. 
Fiscal uncertainty in France has widened sovereign spreads, 
pressuring banks’ bond holdings and funding costs . Although 
French banks represent 13% of the Euro  area Bank Index, volatility 
could spread to regional financial markets. However, stress in the 
broader banking system appears limited , supported by strong 
capital buffers and ECB tools to contain disorderly market moves and 
sovereign market contagion. We will continue to monitor credit 
trends, sovereign spreads and banks’ 2027 earnings guidance. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What has driven the recent weakness in  China equities, 
and what is your outlook from here? 
Our view: We maintain China equities as  a Core holding , as 
valuations remain inexpensive. Rapid technological innovation 
and investment in  AI localisation provide tailwinds, but the 
potential for a re-rating appears delayed. Investors should seek 
to diversify their portfolios and reduce concentration risk. 
Rationale: China equities have weak ened, driven by mounting 
scepticism over AI capex returns, structural margin 
compression and concerns about the country’s significant 
financing requirements. Another notable feature in China has been 
significant share issuance, which has diluted earnings per share and 
weighed on the MSCI China Index’s performance. These pressures 
have been compounded by geopolitical headwinds, restrictions on 
advanced chips, an enduring property sect or contraction and weak 
domestic demand. September’s stimulus measures also 
disappointed markets in terms of their scale and pace of delivery. 
Notwithstanding these headwinds, valuations remain inexpensive, 
supported by rapid domestic innovation and structural tailwinds 
in AI localisation , leading us to retain China equities as a Core 
holding. Elevated volatility reinforces the need to be defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification rather than relying on technology 
sector concentration. We also remain Overweight China’s 
healthcare, financial and materials sectors. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Are you still bullish on US 10-year government bonds?   
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish idea on US 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. The risk -reward 
has become less compelling after the yield broke below 5%, the 
lower bound of our three-month forecast range of 5.00-5.25%. 
Rationale: The US 10 -year yield fell  over 17bps to 4.97% on 22 
October – its first move below 5% since 23 September and biggest 
one-day drop since March 2023. A sell-off in AI-related equities and 
renewed Middle East tensions drove investors towards safe havens. 
Earlier this month, the yield hit its highest level in over two decades. 
The forces behind the rally are fragile, though.  Oil prices remain 
elevated amid escalating Middle East tensions, and September US 
consumer inflation rose to 3.7% y/y . Fed officials  have signalled 
there is no urgency to raise rates at the 27-28 October meeting, 
but persistent price pressures keep further tightening on the table. 
We prefer to lock in gains and close our opportunistic bullish idea on 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

Significant share issuance has kept the market 
capitalisation fairly stable, while the MSCI China 
Index has declined c.20% over the past one year

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for French bank bonds , especially 
ahead of France’s upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews?   
Our view: The recent sell-off in French bank bonds is a repricing 
of tail risks, not a credit story. We expect further price volatility 
until the April 2027 French presidential election, particularly at 
the junior end of the capital structure, so we are closing our 
opportunistic EU bank contingent convertibles (CoCos1) idea at 
a gain of 2.0%. 
Rationale: We believe the recent sell -off in French bank bonds 
reflects domestic political and fiscal risks rather than weaker 
credit fundamentals . The sustained widening of the OAT -Bund 
spread has raised concerns about the profit and loss (P&L) impact 
on banks' sovereign debt holdings. Banks provide limited 
disclosure on these exposures. However, banks’ investment 
portfolios are generally diversified, and holdings booked at amortised 
cost should soften the P&L impact. Near -term catalysts include the 
upcoming sovereign credit rating rev iews. A sovereign downgrade 
could trigger bank rating downgrades, with domestically focused 
lenders potentially more exposed than regionally diversified peers. A 
sovereign credit event is not our base case, but we expect headline 
risks to persist until the  French presidential election . Against this 
backdrop, we are closing our opportunistic EU bank CoCos idea. 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Will the November US mid-term elections and European 
bond volatility support a sustained Swiss franc (CHF) rebound?  
Our view: We expect a near -term CHF rebound, supported 
mainly by safe -haven demand amid European bond volatility. 
We believe the US mid-term elections will be a secondary driver. 
Rationale: European sovereign bond volatility provides the stronger 
catalyst for CHF strength. The Fr ance-Germany government bond 
yield spread remains elevated near 160bps, reflecting heightened 
French fiscal concerns and reinforcing the CHF’s safe-haven appeal. 
The US mid-terms could provide additional support for the CHF. 
A Democratic victory in either chamber is expected to  constrain 
further fiscal expansion, potentially weighing on the USD. However, 
this would not necessarily lead to faster fiscal consolidation. 
The SNB’s 0% policy rate leaves  the CHF at a sizeable interest -
rate disadvantage vs. the USD, constraining sustained CHF gains. 
With EUR/CHF below 0.90 , excessive CHF strength could also 
increase the risk of SNB intervention . A decisive USD/CHF break 
below 0.8130 would strengthen the technical case for a more 
sustained CHF rebound.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

A widening France-Germany 10-year government 
bond yield spread and a stable investment-grade 
(IG) bond option-adjusted spread (OAS) suggest a 
repricing of political and fiscal risk rather than 
credit risk

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would worsening budget deficits and political uncertainty 
across parts of Europe affect your view on EUR/USD and Euro 
area government bond yields? 
Our view: Fiscal and political concerns constrain near -term 
EUR/USD gains, with France being the primary vulnerability. We 
expect EUR/USD to recover towards the 1.17-1.18 range over 
the next  12 months, supported by continued Euro area 
economic growth and an expected fading of USD support. We 
expect wider differentials in Euro area government bond yields, 
with political risk premium elevated in Fr ench government 
bonds (OATs) and German government bonds (Bunds) 
supported by safe-haven demand. 
Rationale: France's high  public debt, large budget deficit and 
political obstacles to fiscal adjustment s are  weighing on market 
confidence, with the country’s  10-year government bond yield 
rising above 5%. The risk of stress spreading to Italy and Spain 
adds to regional concerns, although current evidence does not 
establish a broad European fiscal crisis. German government bonds 
should benefit from safe-haven demand, while fiscal concerns keep 
risk premi a on vulnerable countries' bonds elevated. EUR/USD 
remains more sensitive to US-Germany interest-rate differentials 
and broader market confidence than to French bond yields alone. 
France’s fiscal risks, e levated energy costs and high US interest 
rates weigh on the EUR in the near term. Over the next 12 months, 
Germany’s multi -year public investment programme, resilient 
exports and sound banking-sector fundamentals are expected to 
support Euro area growth. We expect the Fed's rate-hiking cycle to 
peak by mid -2027, reducing USD support and driving  the EUR  
towards 1.17-1.18 . 
The principal tail risk  is fiscal stress widening government bond 
yield spreads and spilling over into bank funding  conditions and 
lending activity, weakening growth and delaying the EUR's recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

France’s fiscal risks constrain near-term EUR/USD 
gains, but a modest recovery remains likely

## Document 455: 「関西の景気動向」９月号 AI関連需要の拡大と関西の製造業生産（PDF：841KB）

Source PDF: all report pdf/「関西の景気動向」９月号　AI関連需要の拡大と関西の製造業生産（PDF：841KB）.pdf
Report source: JRI
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

2026 年９月 
株式会社 日本総合研究所 
調査部 関西経済研究センター 
http://www.jri.co.jp/report/medium/kansai/

１．景気の現状と展望 
 関西の景気は、緩やかな回復基調を維持しつつも、一部に弱い動きがみられる。企業部門では、円安傾向に
加えて、世界的なAI関連需要の拡大を背景に輸出額は増加している。AI関連投資の活発化などを背景に、
鉱工業生産にも持ち直しの動きがみられる。設備投資は、中東情勢の混乱などによる先行き不透明感が意識
されるなかでも、企業の収益計画が高めの水準を維持するもとで拡大基調が続いている。一方、家計部門では、
所得環境の改善が続くものの物価高が重石となり、個人消費の回復ペースは緩慢にとどまっている。 
先行きを展望すると、中東情勢の混乱が長引くなか、資源価格の高止まりが企業活動の重石となる。一方、
世界的な AI 関連需要の拡大が関西の生産や輸出に波及する動きが続き、設備投資の押し上げにもつながる
と見込まれる。個人消費は、持続的な賃上げの動きが下支えに寄与するものの、物価高による下押し圧力が
根強く、回復ペースは引き続き緩慢にとどまる見通しである。

２．個別指標の動き 
（１） 輸出・生産動向 
関西の輸出額（円ベース）は大きく増加している。国・地域別にみると、中国（香港を含む）向けや、アジ
ア（除く中国・香港）向けの伸びが大きく、ともに半導体等電子部品の増加がけん引している。米国向けは振
れを伴いつつも増加傾向にある。品目別にみると、電気機器では半導体等電子部品、一般機械では半導体
等製造装置の増加が顕著にみられ、世界的な AI 関連需要の高まりが押し上げに寄与しているとみられる。金
属及び同製品も先端材料としての需要増や、それに伴う市況の高騰を背景に高水準で推移している。 
一方、輸出を数量面でみると、回復傾向にあるものの、ペースは緩やかにとどまっている。中国向けやアジア
NIEs 向けは低水準での推移が続いているほか、ASEAN 向けも伸び悩んでいる。米国向けは振れを伴いつつ
も、基調としては底堅く推移している。日本銀行が公表している実質輸出指数にも増勢の加速はみられない。

関 西 の 景 気 動 向

### Page 2

2 
以上のように、輸出数量の回復が緩慢にとどまる一方、輸出金額が大幅に増加しており、両者の動きに乖離
がみられる。これは、円安による輸出金額の押し上げや、AI 関連需要の拡大を受けた関連財の価格高騰によ
る影響が大きい。

鉱工業生産は、持ち直しの動きが続いている。業種別にみると、半導体製造装置やデータセンター向け設備
など AI 関連需要の裾野の広がりが追い風となっている生産用機械工業や電気・情報通信機械工業、機能性
素材や医薬品の増産を受けた化学工業の上振れが目立つ。一方、電子部品・デバイス工業は依然として低
水準での推移が続いている（関西における電子部品・デバイス生産の低迷について、詳しくは後述のトピックス
を参照）。

（２） 企業収益・設備投資動向 
企業収益を日本銀行大阪支店「短観」（６月調査、全規模・全産業）でみると、関西企業全体の 2026
年度の経常利益（全産業・全規模）は前年度比▲12.9％と、減益見通しとなった。中東情勢の混乱が長
引くなか資源価格の高止まりなどが収益の下押し圧力となっている。もっとも、2025 年度が同＋16.3％と、価
格転嫁の浸透などを背景に想定を大きく上回って着地したため、その反動減という側面も大きい。コロナ禍前と
の比較では、引き続き高めの水準を維持している。

企業が高水準の収益を見込むもとで、関西企業は積極的な設備投資姿勢を維持している。日本銀行大
阪支店「短観」６月調査による2026年度の設備投資計画（全産業・全規模）は前年度比＋9.7％と、過
去（2010～2024 年度）の平均伸び率を上回る水準にある。機械設備や建設資材の価格上昇などから名
目の投資額が押し上げられている点は割り引く必要があるものの、中東情勢の緊迫化が企業の設備投資マイ
ンドに与える影響は今のところ限定的にとどまっている。ソフトウエア投資も前年度比＋5.1％と増勢が続いてお
り、効率化や省力化を目的とした企業の積極的な投資姿勢に変化はみられない。

（３） 雇用・所得動向 
関西の雇用環境は、人手不足を背景に良好な状態が続いている。雇用関連指標をみると、有効求人倍率
は１倍を超え、完全失業率（季節調整値）は 2％台後半の低位で推移している。雇用者数は高めの水準
を保っている。 
所得面をみると、府県別「毎月勤労統計調査」から推計した４～６月期の関西の一人当たり現金給与総
額は前年同期比＋4.3％と、前期に比べてプラス幅が拡大した。今年の春闘における高い賃上げ水準での妥
結や、夏季賞与の増加（大阪府内企業を対象とする調査では前年比＋2.5％）などを反映し、賃金は高め

の伸びとなった。雇用者報酬を実質ベースでみると、物
価高による下押し圧力の緩和も相まって、4～６月期
は前年同期比＋３％超の増加となった。もっとも、春
先以降の原油高の影響がラグを伴って波及すると予想
されるほか、中東情勢の混乱が長期化するなか、原油
価格は依然として高水準にある。先行き、物価の伸び
は再び拡大する公算が大きく、実質所得には下押し圧
力が強い状況が続く見込みである。

（４） 消費動向 
関西の小売販売額をみると、スーパー、コンビニエンスストア、百貨店の販売額は緩やかに増加している。 
需要側統計（家計調査）をみると、実質ベースで見た消費支出は、4 月に税負担の軽減が実施された自
動車購入の増加などによる一時的な押し上げがみられたものの、均してみれば、物価高への警戒感が根強いも
とで、回復の勢いは鈍い。 
消費者マインドは、中東情勢の混乱直後の大幅
な悪化から改善傾向にあるものの、依然として悪化
前の水準を回復していない。とりわけ、大幅な落ち込
みがみられた「暮らし向き」や「耐久消費財の買い時
判断」の改善が遅れている。地政学リスクの長期化を
背景とした物価高への根強い警戒感が消費者マイン
ドの重石となっている。 
先行きの個人消費は、賃上げの持続が下支えに
寄与するものの、消費者物価の伸びが再び拡大する
もとで、回復ペースは緩慢にとどまる公算が大きい。
